{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_015tcJgKsmSw7QpaYjWE6FnZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Eik88WcYtxz1cHrPoyAjEL","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"autodl-4090","account":"root","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/download_libero.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport os\nimport time\nfrom huggingface_hub import HfApi, hf_hub_download\n\nHF_REPO = \"yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets\"\nLOCAL_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data\"\n\napi = HfApi()\nall_files = api.list_repo_files(HF_REPO, repo_type=\"dataset\")\nhdf5_files = [f for f in all_files if f.endswith(\".hdf5\")]\nprint(f\"Total hdf5 files to download: {len(hdf5_files)}\")\n\nalready = set()\nfor root, dirs, files in os.walk(LOCAL_DIR):\n    for f in files:\n        if f.endswith(\".hdf5\"):\n            rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(root, f), LOCAL_DIR)\n            already.add(rel)\n\nremaining = [f for f in hdf5_files if f not in already]\nprint(f\"Already downloaded: {len(already)}, remaining: {len(remaining)}\")\n\nfor i, fname in enumerate(remaining):\n    for attempt in range(5):\n        try:\n            print(f\"[{i+1}/{len(remaining)}] Downloading {fname} (attempt {attempt+1})...\")\n            hf_hub_download(\n                repo_id=HF_REPO,\n                filename=fname,\n                repo_type=\"dataset\",\n                local_dir=LOCAL_DIR,\n            )\n            print(f\"  OK: {fname}\")\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  FAIL attempt {attempt+1}: {e}\")\n            if attempt < 4:\n                time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n            else:\n                print(f\"  GIVING UP on {fname}\")\n\n# Final count\nfor subdir in [\"libero_10\", \"libero_90\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_spatial\"]:\n    full = os.path.join(LOCAL_DIR, subdir)\n    if os.path.isdir(full):\n        files = [f for f in os.listdir(full) if f.endswith(\".hdf5\")]\n        print(f\"{subdir}: {len(files)} hdf5 files\")\n\nprint(\"SCRIPT_DONE\")\nPYEOF\necho \"script {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01HZfKmXraYadwnqWCygwi66","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VF2ZESH3Zpp6cP4qrSAxWc","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"autodl-4090","account":"root","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/download_libero2.py << 'PYEOF'\nimport os, sys, time\nsys.stdout = open(sys.stdout.fileno(), mode='w', buffering=1)\n\nfrom huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download\n\nHF_REPO = \"yifengzhu-hf/LIBERO-datasets\"\nLOCAL_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/libero_data\"\n\n# Hardcoded file list from repo\nSUBDIRS = {\n    \"libero_10\": [\n        \"KITCHEN_SCENE3_turn_on_the_stove_and_put_the_moka_pot_on_it_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"KITCHEN_SCENE4_put_the_black_bowl_in_the_bottom_drawer_of_the_cabinet_and_close_it_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"KITCHEN_SCENE6_put_the_yellow_and_white_mug_in_the_microwave_and_close_it_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"KITCHEN_SCENE8_put_both_moka_pots_on_the_stove_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"LIVING_ROOM_SCENE1_put_both_the_alphabet_soup_and_the_cream_cheese_box_in_the_basket_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"LIVING_ROOM_SCENE2_put_both_the_alphabet_soup_and_the_tomato_sauce_in_the_basket_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"LIVING_ROOM_SCENE2_put_both_the_cream_cheese_box_and_the_butter_in_the_basket_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"LIVING_ROOM_SCENE5_put_the_white_mug_on_the_left_plate_and_put_the_yellow_and_white_mug_on_the_right_plate_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"LIVING_ROOM_SCENE6_put_the_white_mug_on_the_plate_and_put_the_chocolate_pudding_to_the_right_of_the_plate_demo.hdf5\",\n        \"STUDY_SCENE1_pick_up_the_book_and_place_it_in_the_back_compartment_of_the_caddy_demo.hdf5\",\n    ],\n}\n\n# For libero_90, libero_goal, libero_object, libero_spatial we need the full list\n# Use snapshot_download per-folder approach instead\nfrom huggingface_hub import snapshot_download\n\nos.makedirs(LOCAL_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n\nfolders = [\"libero_10\", \"libero_90\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_spatial\"]\nfor folder in folders:\n    target = os.path.join(LOCAL_DIR, folder)\n    if os.path.isdir(target):\n        existing = [f for f in os.listdir(target) if f.endswith(\".hdf5\")]\n        expected = {\"libero_90\": 90}.get(folder, 10)\n        if len(existing) >= expected:\n            print(f\"SKIP {folder}: already has {len(existing)} files\")\n            continue\n\n    print(f\"Downloading {folder}...\")\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            snapshot_download(\n                repo_id=HF_REPO,\n                repo_type=\"dataset\",\n                local_dir=LOCAL_DIR,\n                allow_patterns=f\"{folder}/*\",\n            )\n            count = len([f for f in os.listdir(os.path.join(LOCAL_DIR, folder)) if f.endswith(\".hdf5\")])\n            print(f\"  {folder}: {count} files downloaded\")\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Attempt {attempt+1} failed: {e}\")\n            if attempt < 2:\n                time.sleep(10)\n    \nprint(\"---FINAL COUNT---\")\nfor folder in folders:\n    target = os.path.join(LOCAL_DIR, folder)\n    if os.path.isdir(target):\n        count = len([f for f in os.listdir(target) if f.endswith(\".hdf5\")])\n        print(f\"{folder}: {count}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"{folder}: MISSING\")\nprint(\"SCRIPT_DONE\")\nPYEOF\necho \"script written\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":388,"cache_read_input_tokens":84466,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":388},"output_tokens":72,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"737dc131-116f-4333-938e-3538640e6946","uuid":"0e609336-2596-4d8d-8d39-93f52600aef3"}
